Abbreviations: EI is Evidence Inference 2.0; ECE is expected calibration error; UTC is Coordinated Universal Time; SHA-256 is Secure Hash Algorithm 256; JSON is JavaScript Object Notation; IDF is inverse document frequency.

## How the check was made

src/eval/check_fiorillo_v0_5_figures.py read the sections How each stage was run, Results (test split) of docs/fiorillo_v0_5.md as written (SHA-256 of the file checked: 3a6b257627c12e2ed2ee9e6b6929236b5be7d3a055ea55f710cba6ca59c2fefb) and found every number in them. It looked for each number's source among session 9's results files (92 found, listed above): every number in them under its file and key, every list's length, the time of day of every time stamp, every number written in a rule's text, and every interval (12,783 sources in all). A number matches a source value when the value, rounded to the number's printed decimals, is the number (they differ by at most half a unit in the last printed place); a percentage is compared with 100 times the value, or with the value where the source holds a percentage, and a time with the time of day of a time stamp. A number printed without thousands separators, or as -0.0000, does not match, since the documents never print one so. An interval (estimate, low to high) or a range (low to high) is matched as one figure to a source that holds its parts.

Which source a number means was found from the words that the source's key shares with the number's context: in a table, its column's heading, its row's labels and the table's title; in prose, the words before it in its clause, the rest of its clause and sentence, the identifiers of its paragraph and its headings. Each shared word is weighted by its nearness and by its rarity among the keys (IDF). A number passes when a source that fits its context at least 0.5 times as well as the best fit prints as it; it does not match when none does and its context names a source clearly (a fit of at least 16.64, what one word next to the number gives when 50 keys hold it), and the source its context names is then given; otherwise it has no source. A sentence or table that names results files of session 9 was searched in those files only.

Readings (the cells of Reading columns, the readings of comparisons in prose after an interval, PASS and FAIL, and quoted readings) were checked against the reading above the sources that their row's or sentence's numbers matched, or else the reading their context names best; a comparison's reading by its meaning (no difference, which side is better, or higher or lower), as compare_session8.py reads the interval, and any other by its words.

Before the document was checked, 14 errors were planted in copies of it, one at a time, and each was reported: line 298, Table 6, `prior`, Log loss, estimate: 1.0767 made 1.0768 (MISMATCH); line 525 (Results (test split)), number 1, estimate: 0.5797 made 0.5798 (MISMATCH); line 719, Table 24, PubMedQA, `gemma4_31b`, Forward seconds a question: 0.8040 made 0.8041 (MISMATCH); line 139, Table 1, prepare, What it did: 5,279 made 5,277 (MISMATCH); line 207 (How each stage was run), number 25: $3.52 made $3.53 (NO SOURCE); line 564 (Results (test split)), number 5: 13:40:26 made 13:40:25 (MISMATCH); line 418, Table 12, `s9_swap_1_2` minus `s9_b_1_2`, Log loss, Difference (95 percent interval), low: -0.0156 made -0.0157 (MISMATCH); line 641, Table 20, `s8_ei` with `gemma4_31b` (Evidence Inference), Confidence rule, estimate: 0.9163 made 0.9162 (MISMATCH); line 375 (Results (test split)), number 3, estimate: 0.0169 made 0.0166 (MISMATCH); line 471, Table 13, `s9_ei_wavg` minus `s9_ei`, F1, significantly increased, System: 0.9185 made 0.9184 (MISMATCH); line 252 (Results (test split)), number 1: 654 made 655 (NO SOURCE); line 173 (How each stage was run), number 1: 1,218 made 1,219 (MISMATCH); line 346, Table 8, `s9_ei_wavg` minus `prior`, Log loss, Reading: lower (better) made no difference was detected (MISMATCH); line 367, Table 9, 1, ECE, Result: PASS made FAIL (MISMATCH).

Limits: a changed number that equals another source value which its context names as clearly would pass; figures that the document labels derived are not computed here, so they show as having no source unless a source holds them; numbers from files that are not among the sources (docs/log.md, the console's pages except as pod_console.json records them, and earlier sessions' results) have no source here; and the zipped stage logs stop where package wrote the logs zip, before its own last lines.

Not counted as numbers, wherever they appear: anything in code type or holding an underscore or a slash (system names, keys, file names); version names and numbers (v0.1 to v0.5, 0.30.0); model names (Qwen3-4B-Base, Gemma 4 31B-it, BioGPT-Large, TabICLv2 and the like); idea numbers (I-01 and so on), G1 to G6, H1 to H4 and K1 to K10; table, figure, correction, criterion, session, step, seed, decision and round numbers; dates, time stamps, the years 2010 to 2029 and eight-digit seeds; ordinals; the phrases "95 percent", "30 days", "80 percent chance", "prints as 0" and "per 1,000"; readmission's levels 0 to 2; sizes such as 4B; and the numbers of numbered lists. Line numbers are the document's.

## Result

2,802 numbers: 2,741 PASS, 0 MISMATCH and 61 NO SOURCE. 105 readings: 105 PASS, 0 MISMATCH and 0 NO SOURCE.
